  branches are reachable (m >= .90; .811 <= m < .90; m < .811). Consequently the section 1.5
  deliverable "a battery reaching all four D3 branches and STOP" is not constructible through the
  section 1.4 selector while s is fixed at .861.

  'c2_firstsign_B1-4' and 'c2_firstsign_B1-4x_random'; no B1-vs-B2-only family exists. Its target
  (targets['c2_firstsign_*']) is "rows B1..B4 (or B1x..B4x) at the read position: +1 for B1, B3" —
  a four-mask probe with classes {B1,B3} vs {B2,B4}, and the measurand is the FIRST-ROUND sign.
  L51-L62 auc_kfold min .9816, max .9924, band MEDIAN .9884. So: the displayed B1 - B2 arithmetic
  is not the class difference of the probe that produced .98-.99; and .861 being a band median,
  the matching recon statistic is the band median .9884, not the range floor ".98".
